# Frozen-data proposal: narrow Rust literal writers

Status: proposed, not registered. No fixture has run. Base: ed65c092e81ad62c54e49aea6573e59f66eeeddd.

This is NEW supplemental scope. The earlier sealed Rust6/18 corpus was not recovered. Preserve the original16-category matrix, parser extensions, trusted-record controls and complete-ledger/worker acceptance.

## Data contract

The JSON records below are documentation data. They are not executable tests or observed checker output. Every normal row gives complete Snapshot entry bytes/modes/blobs, a complete independent manifest, expected native witnesses and diagnostic requirements. Each row stands alone. No source file is executed. UTF-8 strings use JSON escapes; decode them exactly, including BOM/CRLF. Hex bytes are exact. No implicit trailing LF exists beyond the recorded value. Modes are octal digit strings. Index identity is Git SHA-1 of `blob <byte length>\0<bytes>`, calculated during document authoring, not from discovery.

The manifest object must parse from the exact `rust/inventory.json` entry. That entry is UTF-8 JSON with sorted keys, no whitespace between fields and a single final LF. Its own FileRecord has null reviewed_git_blob by the existing schema. Ordinary source hashes are refreshed for every mutation. All omitted Snapshot arrays are explicitly empty.

`native_witnesses` are required observations before a negative can be counted as qualified. Exact call IDs, receipt argument values and literal token spans are independent expectations from source bytes, not candidate-returned facts. Primary owner_fn_byte_range and logical_module witnesses must be checked through a read-only per-invocation test trace of the same discovery path; the ordinary manifest caller_boundary prose is not proof of function ownership. Supported normal rows additionally require exact caller-to-call invokes, call-to-body renders and call-to-output writes-executable edges shown in their manifests. A call receipt's `callee` is `rust:std::fs/write`; its `operation` is write. Empty validation diagnostics are required for every accepted primary row. For refusal rows, all named codes must occur at the relevant source; unrelated bookkeeping diagnostics cannot satisfy them. Additional diagnostics must be reported, not hidden.

N15 deliberately supplies invented complete call/body/output claims from inert text. N16/N17 preserve the real native witness while changing only the declared ledger, except N17f, whose supported Rust literal contains invalid generated shell. N18 supplies a cfg(test)-only per-invocation inspected-producer value into the real private composition path. It does not claim qualification of the compiled record inspector or create runtime authority. The main plan must approve this seam before test code.

## Exact resource encodings

N02 uses exact documentation-level bindings for repeated byte values. This is a lossless representation of complete input data, not candidate-generated expectations. `bytes_utf8` decodes a JSON string to UTF-8; `bytes_rle` concatenates each hex byte sequence exactly its stated repeat count; `bytes_concat` concatenates the listed strings/bindings. An exact `$BODY` value in manifest/receipt templates substitutes the complete UTF-8 string defined by that row's binding. It is not a shell expansion or a runtime variable.

N02b alone uses `$repeat`: expand the inclusive integer interval in ascending order, format i as six decimal digits, replace the exact `{i:06d}` substrings and splice the resulting records into the containing array. Every other template key/value is literal. This fixes the full100001-entry Snapshot and complete manifest without printing100001 identical program records. The fixed self-entry synthetic blob is forty zeroes, with ordinary canonical manifest bytes and the existing null self receipt. This does not claim a real Git snapshot or index qualification.

All resource source lengths, hashes, call/literal ranges, root/module edges and expected manifests are now explicit in the case records below. N02c includes its full bounded shell byte string. N02d includes17 concrete source-hash/receipt rows and a full manifest, with one lossless body binding. Authoring performed byte concatenation/hashing only; no Rust parser, compiler, shell source, discovery, validator or test was run.

-> usize {\n    match fs::read_to_string(path) {\n        Ok(text) => text.lines().count(),\n        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => 0,\n        Err(_) => panic!(\"fixture marker read failed\"),\n    }\n}\n\nfn clear_marker(path: &Path) {\n    if path.exists() {\n        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();\n    }\n}\n\nfn defensive_git(\n    fixture: .stdin(Stdio::piped())\n        .stdout(Stdio::piped())\n        .stderr(Stdio::from(fs::File::open(&fixture.path).unwrap()))\n        .env(\"PATH\", canary_path(directory))\n
